    Food list

    This list is based on the Kyllox fork extract and the restoration code. It is not confirmed data covering the full Nexon original.

    352 foods by shared prototype, plus harvests by variety. The table shows level-60 values (max level if the max is below 60); “Details” shows levels 1·10·20·…·70. Values are for the As obtained(Uncooked) Status; cooking adds the properties of Recipe and changes them (Cooking comparison table). Buffs include the fork's food buff multiplier Changed from original (developer decision).

    Source grade — the badge next to a value shows its basis. Last changed next to a section title is the public version in which that section's content last changed, and links to Patch history.
    • Original (confirmed) A value taken directly from a row of the extracted original data with no signs of editing, or from the original client code
    • Estimated A value the restoration filled in because the original data has no field for it (e.g. rules that were server-only)
    • Changed from original (developer decision) A value that exists in the original but was set differently by developer decision
    • Unverified Values not yet backed by a source — shown in section titles — 8 on this page

    Last changed on this page: 2026.09.29.5

    #Harvest food by variety UnverifiedLast changed 2026.09.29.5

    Harvests that come out under variety names, such as mango, banana and Wild Rose. They keep the shared ingredient category, so they work in existing cooking recipes. Wild Rose's cure link is a restoration rule made at the developer's request and applies to new harvests only.
